# modus — PENDING-ARMS (the W81 ledger: built ≠ armed)

> Anything created-but-not-{merged, installed, propagated, armed} lands here at SHIP+ARM and is
> re-read at TRIAGE of every subsequent modus run. Remove a line ONLY when the arming is PROVEN
> live (a probe of the work, never an exit code). Distinguish legitimate firebreaks (operator
> gate, Legge 5, business decision) from tech debt — say which it is.
>
> Format: `opened YYYY-MM-DD | artifact | missing arming step | owner (me|operator[<categoria>]) | proof-of-armed`
>
> **No phantom operator lane** (Zero, 2026-07-06: "io sono te — non c'è nessun operatore"): owner
> `operator` è valido SOLO con una categoria operatore-vera dichiarata inline — `operator[physical]`,
> `[gui]`, `[tcc]`, `[consent]`, `[secret]`, `[control-plane]`, `[business]`. Tutto il lavoro
> repo/infra eseguibile da una sessione si scrive `me (sessione <macchina>)`. Un owner `operator`
> nudo viene urlato PHANTOM-OPERATOR da `scripts/pending_arms_report.py` e boccia la CI
> (immune-enforcement strict-phantom gate).

Root cause found this session (not a network flake, not credentials, not HTTP/1.1-vs-2, not payload chunking): `git filter-repo` rewrites the hash of EVERY commit in the rewritten branch (normal DAG cascade, not a bug) — zero objects are shared with `origin/main`, so ANY force-push (even a 10%-oldest chunk) must transfer a slice of 142,999 all-new objects / ~2.18GB, and every such attempt on this network stalls (`POST git-receive-pack (chunked)` never completes; isolated via: `curl` raw POST to the same endpoint succeeds in 0.5s, so it's specifically `git send-pack`'s chunked-body generation/transfer that hangs, not GitHub's endpoint or auth). PII risk from this: LOW-not-ZERO — the 2 dossier files + 8 paths are already removed from HEAD (PR #2329, #2332, merged, verified absent) so nobody browsing the repo today sees them; the residual exposure is only for someone who clones/fetches full history. Zero decision 2026-07-13: stop for now, do not keep retrying the same command | next session should NOT retry plain `git push --force` from this network — try either (a) GitHub support-assisted large force-push, (b) a different network/connection, or (c) splitting into a brand-new orphan-history repo + redirect, never blind chunking again (proven ineffective: chunking still transfers all-new objects) | owner: operator[business]-or-next-session-with-new-strategy | proof: `refs/heads/main` in `/private/tmp/claude-501/.../scratchpad/nuzantara-real-purge-v3` verified 0 hits for all 11 targets, 142999 objects/2181182 KB pack, ready to push once a working transfer method is found

- opened 2026-07-16 | pre-push DB clone did not inherit the template's owner — the gate armed last night (#2489) was blocking EVERY push on M5 (fix committed, PR pending). `CREATE DATABASE ... TEMPLATE` re-parents the clone to the creating role (the OS user, `balizero`), and since PG15 `public` is owned by pg_database_owner → resolves to the DB owner → a template owned by `test` loses test's CREATE on public inside the clone. Table ownership is copied; a privilege held implicitly THROUGH ownership is not (an explicit GRANT would have been). 39 failed + 79 errors of 17339, all "permission denied for schema public" + its "relation does not exist" cascade. Latent from #2489 until nuzantara_test was re-created with owner=test at 11:38 today — between two of my own pushes that passed, which is why it looked like my markdown-only commit had broken pytest. Class #2/#3: a fresh guard failing closed on innocent work; and #9, since ownership is a PROXY for the privilege, not the privilege. The local template got an explicit `GRANT USAGE, CREATE ON SCHEMA public TO test` (survives cloning, unlike ownership) as the immediate unblock — that is LOCAL M5 state, so Pro/Mini or any re-bootstrap still needs the hook fix. NOT yet armed fleet-wide: git resolves hooksPath against the MAIN checkout, so this bites only once merged AND pulled on each machine. | me (sessione M5) | proof-of-armed: on a clean template owned by `test` with no explicit grant, the OLD clone path gives owner=balizero / test CREATE=false / `ERROR: permission denied for schema public` and the NEW path gives owner=test / CREATE=true / `CREATE TABLE` — plus the three suites that failed the blocked push (crm/partners, compliance alerts_engine, war_room migration_112) at 69 passed against a clone; fleet-armed when `git log -1 .husky/pre-push` on Pro and Mini shows this commit
- closed 2026-07-16 | pre-push gate did NOT run from agent worktrees — RESOLVED at the root (PR #2510). `core.hooksPath` is the RELATIVE path `.husky/_`, which git resolves against EACH working tree; `_` is husky's GENERATED shim dir (`npm install` makes it in the main checkout) and `git worktree add` never carries untracked files over. So a worktree resolved hooksPath to nothing and every push from it skipped pre-push entirely: no banner, no suite, exit 0 — the worst shape a gate can fail in (superscar #2: green because NOTHING RAN), landing exactly where CLAUDE.md confines all agent work. Cure: `agent_start.py` symlinks `.husky/_` into every new worktree, and `.husky/_` joins BROKER*GENERATED_FILES for the same reason node_modules did (else the broker's own symlink reads `?? .husky/*`and makes --cleanup a no-op forever — curing the gate must not re-open the reaper bug). | proof-of-armed: DONE —`git rev-parse --git-path hooks` resolves in main and to nothing in a worktree; three probe pushes from two worktrees ran no gate (one byte-identical in form to a push that HAD run the suite); symlink the dir in and the same push fires the banner + full suite; PR #2510's own push was gated (17174 passed). 3 tests confirmed RED pre-fix (reach + innocence pinning the no-trailing-slash porcelain shape of a symlink-vs-dir + guilt that an edit to tracked .husky/pre-push is still WIP), 46/46 green.

All 41 unshimmed M5 worktrees backfilled (4 already had it via a stray npm install), 45/45 now resolve the gate. The mid-flight-suite worry was moot: adding a symlink runs nothing — a push does, and only when the sibling chooses to push. Proven, not assumed:`\_worktree_has_wip`returns False on a freshly-shimmed worktree (exemption holds, reaper safe) AND a throwaway push from a backfilled worktree (wr2-b13-worktree-gc) printed the pre-push banner and started the suite (gate fires). Fresh value generated via `openssl rand` (never printed), written to the gitignored 0600 `apps/bali-intel-scraper/.env` (the stack had NO DB_PASSWORD in .env → it ran on the weak `postgres` default, which WAS the exposure). W97 CAVEAT (read the OUTPUT, not the exit code): the stock postgres image `pg_hba.conf` TRUSTS loopback (`host 127.0.0.1/32 trust`), so a naive same-container psql never actually checks the password — old, new AND baseline all return `SELECT 1` (a false-clean proof; the first two attempts this session hit exactly this). The harness must FORCE `scram-sha-256` on host rules for the rotation to be observable. With scram enforced (2 host rules): baseline OLD/TCP = `[1]` (pre-ALTER), NEW/TCP = `[1]` (post-ALTER), and OLD/TCP = `[FATAL: password authentication failed for user "postgres"]` — the rotation proof is the OLD FAILING, exactly as Zero required. Structural rotation (the real path for this ephemeral no-volume container): the 4 cleartext `:-` defaults are gone and `:?` hard-errors bring-up if DB_PASSWORD is unset, so the weak `postgres` default can never be re-selected. Re-ground / re-STOP gate (re-verified this session): live scraper `config/settings.py` targets DB_HOST=localhost DB_PORT=5432 (homebrew postgres@17) — NOT the container's 5433; `docker compose` plugin is NOT installed on Pro; no `bali_intel` docker volume; 5433 free (container down) → ZERO prod/remote consumer of this container credential → re-STOP NOT triggered. | owner: me (sessione Pro) | PROVEN 2026-07-19: reproducible ALTER-ROLE rotation (OLD→auth-fail under scram, NEW→SELECT 1); compose 0 `:-` cleartext defaults (blob-verified on branch). Residuals (both optional, non-blocking): (1) `.env.example` documentation of the now-required DB_PASSWORD/GRAFANA_PASSWORD is operator-only (`.env*` is off-limits/guardrail-protected); (2) a dedicated non-superuser monitoring role could replace `postgres` in the exporter DSN — hardening, not exposure
